With no far-baseline candidate NO template can match, clean input or not. So clay is a true-candidate RECALL failure, not a clutter failure, and the 0/40 was measuring the wrong lever. Successor work is far-baseline RECALL, not decluttering. |

The arithmetic was correct and I verified it myself before endorsing it: `parallel < 1.8 or orthogonal < 1.6` on image-space direction dot products does demand within 25.8 deg of image-parallel and 11.5 deg of image-perpendicular, and a paint at a 70 deg image angle would score orthogonal 1.32 and be rejected. What was WRONG was the unstated premise -- that ordinary broadcast perspective produces baseline-to-lane image angles outside that band. On the audited sample it does not; real sideline framing keeps the paint near enough to square that the gate accepts it. **So basketball has ONE blocker, not two.** G84 stands unchallenged (all four paint lines present among the candidates in 0 of 33 human-solvable frames) and the gate is exonerated. CONSEQUENCE FOR G86: the work is purely DETECTION and the gate needs no change -- do not spend a lane making it perspective-invariant, because it is not the thing that is failing. WORTH RECORDING AS A METHOD LESSON: a confident, code-derived, arithmetically-verified prediction from a strong model, endorsed by the orchestrator after independent checking, was still falsified by twelve hand-marked frames. Verified arithmetic plus an unverified premise is still a guess, and the cheap real-geometry test is what caught it. |

Fix: `_finish` copies decoded_frames, source_resolution and fresh_solves into the ledger row, and compares against the previous run for that sport so a step change is visible at write time. Purely additive; no threshold and no verdict logic involved. |

| REACH | all | **CONSOLIDATED FOUR-SPORT REACHABILITY VERDICT, orchestrator, 2026-09-02.** Five rows measured the same question with the same method on seeded eye-checked frame samples, and the answers agree: **broadcast footage does not support a court_feet solve for four of five sports, and the constraint is the CAMERA, not the code.** SOCCER (G91, G101): canonical point landmarks visible >=4 in 0/100 frames, named lines >=4 in 0/100, and never more than TWO independent pitch directions. FOOTBALL (G95, G106): 17/60 frames expose two independent directions, NONE exposes a third, and 0/60 supplies a valid absolute-yard reference, so even a converging solve could not know which yard line it is on. BASEBALL (G104): reachable only on rare overhead whole-infield shots, **1/120 frames = 0.8 pct**; the ordinary centre-field view reaches at most two points. BASKETBALL: no reachability census yet, only detector numbers -- candidate precision 11.22 pct (G84), all four paint lines co-present in 0/33 frames, and the gate exonerated (G87, 11/12 true paints pass); its recall is still blocked. TENNIS is the sole sport that reaches court_feet today (G47: 0/15 contract rejections). WHY THIS MATTERS MORE THAN ANY SINGLE ROW: a planar homography needs four point correspondences or four independent line constraints. Three sports cap at TWO independent directions no matter how good the detector gets, so 'improve the detector' is not a lever that exists for them. This verdict retires the build-a-solver branch for soccer and football and reduces baseball to a rare-view special case. NOT VERIFIED: basketball has no reachability census; every number here is a per-frame visibility census, not a solved homography. | **UPDATED 2026-09-02 by G111, and it INVERTS the priority.** The row above listed basketball as uncensused. It is now censused and it is the OUTLIER IN THE GOOD DIRECTION: **basketball court_feet is geometrically reachable in 147 of 220 seeded frames = 66.8 pct**, through four visible paint-corner points. Compare soccer 0/100, football a third direction in 0/60, baseball 1/120 = 0.8 pct. So the consolidated verdict's headline -- broadcast footage does not support court_feet -- holds for soccer, football and baseball but NOT for basketball. **Basketball is the one sport where 'improve the detector' is a lever that actually exists**, because the geometry IS on screen in two thirds of frames and what fails is finding it: G84 measured candidate precision at 11.22 pct over 1,764 audited candidates with all four paint lines co-present in 0 of 33 frames, and G87 exonerated the parallel/orthogonal gate at 11 of 12 true paints passing. G115 is measuring the missing recall half now. NOTE THE ROUTE DIFFERENCE, which is actionable: G111 reaches four constraints through paint-corner POINTS, while the existing detector works on LINES and then intersects them. A corner-first detector may be the shorter path and it has never been tried. PRIORITY: calibration effort belongs on basketball, not on soccer or football, both of which are closed at limit. NOT VERIFIED: 66.8 pct is per-frame geometric visibility, not a solved homography, and no basketball clip has been promoted to court_feet. **EXPOSED 2026-09-02 by G117, correction routed to G125.** The baseball figure in this verdict (1/120 = 0.8 pct, G104) has a CONTAMINATED DENOMINATOR: G117 eye-confirmed and quarantined 12 KBO studio and statistics clips at 0/5 live-action frames each, and 40 of G104's 120 sampled frames come from two of them. A studio desk cannot show an infield, so those frames are guaranteed non-reachable and drag the rate down mechanically. The published 0.8 pct is wrong in a KNOWN DIRECTION -- too low. To be fair to G104, it transparently retained 60 of 120 frames as non-game content; what it did not do is exclude them from the reachability denominator. G125 recomputes it. ALSO FROM G117 AND WORTH ITS OWN NOTE: scripts/platformkit/footage_content_gate.py ACCEPTED ALL 12 studio clips, so the existing gate does not detect studio programming and the fix is not 'apply the gate we already have'. The soccer, football and basketball verdicts are NOT known to be exposed -- G113 measured non-baseball live-action at 93.3 pct [86.9, 96.7] -- but that is an expectation, not a measurement of those three samples, and G125 checks it. **UNDER AUDIT 2026-09-02, and the orchestrator flags this as prominently as it promoted it.** G121 tried to label pixel targets for G111's visible corners and REFUSED, reporting that G111's retained 'visible' labels CONFLICT with its own committed source renders -- it names a mid-court view where neither paint rectangle is in the image and a player close-up with no paint rectangle, both carrying visible-corner labels. It fabricated nothing. **The 66.8 pct basketball reachability figure, and therefore the reprioritisation of the whole calibration programme onto basketball, is provisional until G126 reports.** G121 is explicit that it cannot tell whether (a) G111's LABELS are wrong, which would inflate 66.8 pct and invalidate the priority change, (b) the LABEL-TO-RENDER ASSOCIATION is wrong, which would leave 66.8 pct entirely intact and break only the artefact wiring, or (c) G121 misread the manifest. Those have opposite consequences and G126 separates them with a blind source-decoded re-judgement. PRECEDENT for not assuming the labels are fine: G76 measured a basketball paint criterion at 68.6 pct raw agreement and G85 measured a tennis one at 75.0 pct. WHAT IS NOT AFFECTED: G115's line-recall 25/68 = 36.76 pct and its cross-check against G84 rest on the G84/G115 visibility labels, not on G111, so G120 and G123 stand regardless. G119's corner route depends on G111 and is blocked either way. **BASEBALL FIGURE CORRECTED 2026-09-02 by G125: 1/120 = 0.8 pct [0.15, 4.57] becomes 1/80 = 1.3 pct [0.22, 6.75]** after excluding the 40 named G117-quarantined studio rows. The conclusion is unchanged -- reachability is rare -- and G125 is careful that 1/80 is still not a pure game-footage rate. **The OTHER THREE censuses are now verified CLEAN of this contamination**: G101 soccer (100 frames), G106 football-only (60; its 48 soccer rows were already excluded) and G111 basketball (220) carry no studio or non-game programme exposure. That matters for the G126 audit: whatever is wrong with G111's corner labels, it is NOT studio-frame contamination. **CORRECTED 2026-09-02 by the G126 audit: the basketball figure was WRONG and is roughly HALVED. 66.8 pct -> an estimated 33.8 pct.** The audit made its blind judgement from the SOURCE clips before opening any label, and the three-way comparison is unambiguous. Source versus committed render: **45/45 agreement**, so the renders are faithful and the label-to-render association hypothesis is RULED OUT. Source versus G111 label: **22/45 = 48.9 pct, Wilson [35.0, 63.0]** -- G111's visibility labels agree with the actual frames less than half the time. Verdict (a): the labels are wrong. **THE ORCHESTRATOR PROMOTED 66.8 pct INTO THIS VERDICT AND USED IT TO REPRIORITISE THE WHOLE CALIBRATION PROGRAMME ONTO BASKETBALL, AND THREE LANES WERE DISPATCHED ON IT.** WHAT SURVIVES: the DIRECTION of that call. At 33.8 pct basketball is still far above soccer (0/100), football (a third direction in 0/60) and baseball (1/80 = 1.3 pct), so it remains the only sport where detector work is a lever that exists, and G115's line-recall 25/68 = 36.76 pct and its G84 cross-check never depended on G111 at all. WHAT DOES NOT: the magnitude, and any claim built on two-thirds of frames being reachable. 33.8 pct is itself a REWEIGHTED ESTIMATE from a 45-frame audit, not a re-census, so it carries its own uncertainty and must not be quoted as precise -- a full re-census is G130. THIRD DATA POINT IN A PATTERN: visibility criteria in this repo keep measuring unreliable -- G76 basketball paint 68.6 pct, G85 tennis ball 75.0 pct, and now G111 corners at 48.9 pct, the worst yet. Any future row resting on eye labels must measure their agreement before resting on them. **REPLACEMENT NUMBER LANDED 2026-09-02 by G136: basketball reachability is 97/210 = 46.2 pct, Wilson 95 pct [39.6, 52.9], AND IT IS EXPLICITLY CAVEATED.** The blind second pass was committed at 62c30f54f BEFORE the join at 1423506fb, and the ancestry was verified, so the blindness ordering is auditable rather than asserted. Blind reachability agreement is **28/42 = 66.7 pct [51.6, 79.0]** and exact corner-count agreement 27/42 = 64.3 pct. Because agreement sits below 80 pct, the memo treats 46.2 pct as a caveated census estimate rather than a precise reachability claim, which is the correct handling and it is recorded here the same way. TRAJECTORY OF THIS ONE NUMBER, worth keeping visible as a caution: G111 claimed 66.8 pct, the G126 audit measured G111's labels at 22/45 = 48.9 pct against source frames and retracted it, G126's reweighted estimate was 33.8 pct, and a full 210-frame source-decoded re-census with its own reliability measured lands at 46.2 pct. FOURTH DATA POINT IN THE LABEL-RELIABILITY PATTERN: G76 paint 68.6 pct, G85 tennis ball 75.0 pct, G111 corners 48.9 pct, G136 corners 66.7 pct. No eye criterion in this repo has yet cleared 80 pct agreement, so every future row resting on eye labels must measure that agreement inside the row rather than after it. WHAT DOES NOT CHANGE: basketball remains the only sport where detector work is a lever that exists -- 46.2 pct against soccer 0/100, football a third direction in 0/60 and baseball 1/80 = 1.3 pct. **PROGRAMME-LEVEL CLOSURE 2026-09-02, after eleven basketball rows and five reachability censuses.** Broadcast-footage calibration is NOT achievable with the current detection stack for any sport except tennis, and each leg of that is measured rather than assumed. SOCCER unreachable: >=4 point landmarks in 0/100 frames, >=4 named lines in 0/100, never more than two independent directions (G91, G101). FOOTBALL unreachable: two directions in 17/60, a third in 0/60, an absolute-yard reference in 0/60 (G95, G106). BASEBALL 1/80 = 1.3 pct, overhead infield only (G104, corrected by G125). BASKETBALL is the one where the GEOMETRY IS PRESENT -- 46.2 pct [39.6, 52.9] of frames (G136, caveated at 66.7 pct label agreement) -- but neither detection route recovers it: the line route makes 1 of 84 roles available and assigns 0 of 84 correctly (G138), and a naive corner detector scores recall 0/68 and precision 0/1,700 (G141). TENNIS remains the sole sport reaching court_feet (G47, 0/15 contract rejections). CONSEQUENCE FOR THE PROGRAMME: the binding constraint is not solver design and not thresholds -- it is that broadcast framing plus this detector does not deliver four constraints. Effort belongs on tennis, which works, and on acquisition, not on further solver or threshold work for the other four sports. NOT CLOSED: a learned or template-based corner detector is untested; G140's 68 pixel targets and their measured precision floor (p90 11.39 px) are committed and reusable as ground truth for any such attempt. **THREE OVER-READS IN THIS ROW CORRECTED 2026-09-02, found by a Fable review the orchestrator commissioned and then verified against the artefacts.** (1) **'TENNIS WORKS' IS WRONG.** Tennis is the only sport reaching the court_feet COORDINATE CONTRACT (G47, 0 of 15 rejections), but the pod daemon ledger shows **39 tennis rows and 0 passes** -- zero end-to-end. Worse, G34 measured tennis rally share at **41.7 pct (125/300, Wilson [0.362, 0.473])**, which caps achievable whole-clip decoded-frame coverage, and the harness does NOT use a decoded-frame denominator, so coverage is inflated **2.5x to 4.9x on four tennis clips**. The 0.90 coverage bar is therefore unreachable on a whole tennis broadcast as a property of the footage, and it needs its own adjudication -- a scoped rally-range verdict or an explicit CLOSED AT LIMIT -- not the silent optimism this row carried. Accurate statement: the classical solver works on SELECTED RALLY-VIEW RANGES, which is real but far narrower than 'tennis works'. (2) **BASEBALL WAS UNDER-READ.** It is not simply 1.3 pct unreachable: G69/G72 LANDED a scoped metric_local rung, accepted for baseball, NPB and KBO with calibration=mound_lateral_px_per_ft, and G10 reached it on 4 real MLB broadcasts. Baseball has a working partial rung that this row omitted. (3) **'REDIRECT TO ACQUISITION' IS NOT ACTIONABLE** and should not be staffed as a workstream: G142 found no steerable driver, G27's re-ingest failed closed, and G09 found 9 of 11 alternative sources licence-blocked. It is a standing eye on licensing, nothing more. ALSO WORTH RECORDING AGAINST THE BASKETBALL PARK: G31 trained a learned tennis keypoint model on about 2,000 pseudo-labels -- an easier court with far more labels -- and scored PCK@7px 0.077 with a solve proxy of exactly 0.0 on both held-out folds. A learned basketball corner detector from G140's 68 targets, with a tolerance that can never go below the measured 11.39 px label floor, has poor odds; park it with the ground truth committed rather than attempt it.

stop, signal or interrupt that daemon.** G309 is READ-ONLY on the pod and takes NO GPU lease; G310
takes the pod GPU under a lease SERIALIZED with the daemon, gated on free VRAM >= 8,192 MiB.
**Both rows are image-space only (`coordinate_space = image_px`): neither may make a court,
registration or harness-pass claim, and neither may touch the ledger `passed` field.**

CHANGE (additive, safe area): an `ffprobe` fallback inside `probe_source()` plus one new `source_probe` provenance key (`cv2` / `ffprobe` / `none`), every existing key keeping its name and its cv2-success value; plus a backfill script writing recovered values to a NEW sidecar JSONL keyed by `game_id`. **THE LEDGER IS APPEND-ONLY AND NO HISTORICAL ROW IS REWRITTEN** -- earlier whole-file rewrites destroyed 17 rows across 9 gap ids. Bar: **0 segments with empty source metadata among those whose source video still exists on the pod, counting a segment covered when the UNION of its ledger row and the sidecar carries all three; segments whose video is gone are the row's LIMIT and are reported by count and id, never dropped from the denominator**; n = every ledger segment row (CENSUS); reproduction = a second independent `ffprobe -show_streams` on 3 named segments returning byte-identical values. The pod `cv2.__version__` is printed: an all-None probe from a cv2 5 bump (2026-09-02 landmine) is a DIFFERENT defect from an unreadable file and the row must say which it measured. **Filling a metadata field is NOT a tracking improvement: no recall, precision, coverage, registration or harness-pass claim, and no `passed` flip.** | `specs/G317_spec.md`; `S314_teach_packet_census_2026-09-07.md` | **DONE 9dbce63b0 -- PREMISE FALSIFIED (ACCEPT WITH CORRECTIONS).** 0 of 58 pod ledger rows (25 segment rows, CENSUS) are missing `source_fps` / `source_height` / `source_duration`; the spec premise came from S314's LOCAL old-schema CSV finding, a different denominator; `probe_source` and `track_daemon` byte-identical (no code change, no backfill, no `passed` flip).
